2026-10-31T00:29:03.6677797Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 10 (KV sent and polled one period earlier at stages 1-3) with a fresh image

The round that run 41148979897 tried to serve, now with a build.
That run stopped in provisioning: the PP4 CPU gate accepts only its pinned image
or an image built from the commit, and pp4-loop-latency.patch is a build
overlay. No GPU time was used.

One image build and one PP4 serving run at c232. The previous official best is
9883.36 (41073991312). Round 9 (41135856718) was neutral, with an unofficial
9883.51: AIPerf aborted while exporting after the run.

What changed since round 8:
1. 8d26550: stage-0 admission before the Decode bootstrap. Neutral in round 9;
   kept.
2. c76919f: in the PP4 loop, stages 1-3 take their microbatch result and send
   its KV before the next launch, as only the last stage did before. Every
   stage now polls the KV transfers after its launch, not at the top of the
   iteration.
   - Why: stage 2 sent its KV one period (112-120 ms) after stages 0, 1 and 3,
     in every worker of rounds 8 and 9.
   - Every release came four iterations after its result, one of them only
     because the poll ran right after the send.
   - The router streams the first token only when Prefill completes, so the
     release time is the client's first token.
   - The scheduler model in scripts/check-pp4-direct-loop.py reproduces the
     Firmus timeline on the round-9 source and passes on the new one.
   - Expected at the start burst: first tokens about 0.115 s earlier, about 6
     more requests under 3 s, +0.10%.
   - The GPU work is unchanged.

What this run should show in the existing logs:
- the PP2 Prefill-batch line within about 0-20 ms of PP0 (now 112-120 ms)
- Prefill 200 OK about 0.35 s after the PP0 result (now 0.466 s)
- the PP0 period unchanged at 114.9 / 114.6 / 118.3 ms

Job time about 10 min of 17.

Validation: full pytest suite, the PP4 CPU loop checks, ruff.
